## Workflow

Verify identity, exact scope, sources and representative GPS, four worldwide peers, five independently justified V2 dimensions; write YAML and individual review, regenerate rankings, run validators and benchmark checks. Unknown fields stay null. No national quotas. Update queue as records land. This branch reproduces deterministic ranking row format and tied ranks for one hundred and forty additions; Python generator and full validation not executed (local network unavailable). No GitHub Actions used.


### Integration checkpoint — 2026-11-26

Merged main snapshot `d6da3f69e64e73e7e150180cc8e17d9db27c9502` into the audit branch, preserving all 113 main changes and all existing audit YAML files. Reconciled two generated rankings by taking main's rows for existing records and inserting audit-only YAML records: Egypt 42 ranked / 42 total; Tanzania 24 ranked / 30 total. Full Python generator, diagnostics refresh, and independent factual verification remain pending.


### Pass 20 — African verification and integration

Ten pass-15 coordinates replaced with published map references and more specific visitor sources. Ratings unchanged; the review evidence status remains source-linked-unverified. Latest inspected main snapshot 311fbba9e001cb296d54409eee0dd372b6efba7d incorporated (27 nonoverlapping changed files). Review heading/dimension format corrected for the 17 audit additions in TZ/MG/EG/MA; full Python workflow report still pending.


### Pass 21 — Review normalization and generated-row consistency

Normalized the remaining 118 audit-added reviews (135 including the 17 from pass 20) to the repository's level-two heading and capitalized dimension convention. Compared all 135 new ranking rows against YAML-derived scores, coordinates, category ordering, name search URLs and the generator's current name-based type rules using a connector-side implementation. Five earlier type mismatches corrected, plus 1 in the African cohort. This is not a run of the full Python generator. Known limitation: type inference is lexical; Cathedral Cove is classified as a religious site, despite being a natural attraction. Château des Baux and Blue Grotto fall into the generic Attraction bucket. Improve inference with separate regression tests rather than overriding generated rows manually. The public-runner validation report has not appeared; full schema/benchmark/review checks and diagnostics refresh are still outstanding. No YAML source records or ratings modified in pass 21. Main snapshot ce496658ec484f60b84f978e50078442221d4e42 integrated.

### Fast assessment policy — effective after pass 141 (2026-11-27)

User-directed priority: estimate sub-90 sights from overview or existing knowledge; reserve detailed individual source research and calibration for plausible 90+ overall scores and proposed 9.5+ individual fields. Do not suppress a plausible high score to avoid research. Lower scores remain explicitly provisional, with unverified coordinates or references labeled accordingly. Use existing global scale and schema; check duplicates, scope, numerical scores and rankings. Existing validation and verification requirements remain open; fast assessments are not independently verified audits. Aim for 20–40 candidates per pass where practical. Temporary access restrictions do not reduce intrinsic quality. No geographic quotas or changes to weights.
